--- a/notes/chores-3.md
+++ b/notes/chores-3.md
@@ -249,3 +249,306 @@ a=[fx]
 
 **Conclusion:** Square brackets are safe unquoted in `tspec ts set key+=[values]`
 syntax. No quoting needed for normal use.
+
+## 20260211 - Orthogonal `ts set`/`add`/`remove` with separate key and value args
+
+### Problem
+
+`ts set` takes the entire assignment as a single positional argument (`key=value`),
+which forces users to fight the shell:
+
+```bash
+# Scalar — fine
+tspec ts set rustc.lto=true
+
+# Array — bracket/quote gymnastics
+tspec ts set 'linker.args=["-static","-nostdlib"]'
+
+# Append — still awkward
+tspec ts set 'linker.args+=-Wl,--gc-sections'
+```
+
+The root cause is two-fold:
+
+1. **Key and value crammed into one shell token** — any special characters in
+   the value (brackets, quotes, spaces) force quoting the entire `key=value`.
+2. **Overloaded operators (`=`, `+=`, `-=`) encoded in a string** — three
+   fundamentally different operations (replace, append, remove) masquerade as
+   modes of one command via string parsing.
+
+### Design: Orthogonal verbs with separate args
+
+Split into four commands on two axes:
+
+| Axis | Command | Purpose |
+|---|---|---|
+| Field-level | `ts set` | Set scalar or replace entire array |
+| Field-level | `ts unset` | Remove entire field (already exists) |
+| Item-level | `ts add` | Add items to array (append or insert) |
+| Item-level | `ts remove` | Remove items from array (by value or index) |
+
+Key and value are separate clap positional arguments. Array values are variadic
+(each element is its own shell arg — no brackets, no inner quotes).
+
+### Hyphen-prefixed values and `allow_hyphen_values`
+
+Values like `-static` and `-nostdlib` start with `-`, which clap normally tries
+to parse as flags. The fix is `#[arg(allow_hyphen_values = true)]` on the
+`value` field — clap then treats unknown hyphen-prefixed tokens as positional
+values. This means **no `--` needed** in normal use:
+
+```bash
+tspec ts set linker.args -static -nostdlib      # just works
+tspec ts add linker.args -Wl,--gc-sections      # just works
+```
+
+The only edge case is a value that exactly matches a defined short flag (`-p`,
+`-t`, `-i`). In practice no real linker/rustc flag is just `-p` or `-t`, but
+`--` remains available as an escape hatch for that theoretical collision.
+
+### Syntax
+
+```bash
+# === Field-level (set/unset) ===
+
+# Scalars — just two words
+tspec ts set rustc.lto true
+tspec ts set cargo.profile release
+tspec ts set panic abort
+
+# Replace entire array — each element is a separate arg
+tspec ts set linker.args -static -nostdlib
+tspec ts set rustc.build_std core alloc
+
+# Remove entire field
+tspec ts unset linker.args
+
+# === Item-level (add/remove) ===
+
+# Append (default add behavior)
+tspec ts add linker.args -Wl,--gc-sections
+tspec ts add rustc.flags -Cforce-frame-pointers=yes -Clink-dead-code=no
+
+# Insert at position
+tspec ts add -i 0 linker.args -nostdlib
+
+# Remove by value
+tspec ts remove linker.args -static
+tspec ts remove linker.args -static -nostdlib
+
+# Remove by index
+tspec ts remove -i 2 linker.args
+```
+
+### The six array operations
+
+| # | Operation | Command | Example |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| 1 | Replace all | `ts set` | `ts set linker.args -static -nostdlib` |
+| 2 | Clear field | `ts unset` | `ts unset linker.args` |
+| 3 | Append items | `ts add` | `ts add linker.args -Wl,--gc-sections` |
+| 4 | Insert at position | `ts add -i N` | `ts add -i 0 linker.args -nostdlib` |
+| 5 | Remove by value | `ts remove` | `ts remove linker.args -static` |
+| 6 | Remove by index | `ts remove -i N` | `ts remove -i 2 linker.args` |
+
+### Clap definitions
+
+```rust
+/// Set a field (scalar value or replace entire array)
+Set {
+    /// Field key (e.g., "rustc.lto", "linker.args")
+    key: String,
+    /// Value(s). For scalars, one value. For arrays, each arg is an element.
+    #[arg(required = true, allow_hyphen_values = true)]
+    value: Vec<String>,
+    #[arg(short = 'p', long = "package")]
+    package: Option<String>,
+    #[arg(short = 't', long = "tspec")]
+    tspec: Option<String>,
+}
+
+/// Add items to an array field (append by default, or insert at position)
+Add {
+    /// Field key (must be an array field)
+    key: String,
+    /// Items to add
+    #[arg(required = true, allow_hyphen_values = true)]
+    value: Vec<String>,
+    /// Insert at this index instead of appending
+    #[arg(short = 'i', long = "index")]
+    index: Option<usize>,
+    #[arg(short = 'p', long = "package")]
+    package: Option<String>,
+    #[arg(short = 't', long = "tspec")]
+    tspec: Option<String>,
+}
+
+/// Remove items from an array field (by value or by index)
+Remove {
+    /// Field key (must be an array field)
+    key: String,
+    /// Items to remove by value (not used with --index)
+    #[arg(allow_hyphen_values = true)]
+    value: Vec<String>,
+    /// Remove item at this index instead of by value
+    #[arg(short = 'i', long = "index")]
+    index: Option<usize>,
+    #[arg(short = 'p', long = "package")]
+    package: Option<String>,
+    #[arg(short = 't', long = "tspec")]
+    tspec: Option<String>,
+}
+```
+
+### Validation rules
+
+- `ts set` on a scalar field: exactly one value required
+- `ts set` on an array field: one or more values (replaces entire array)
+- `ts add`: key must be an array field (error on scalar)
+- `ts add -i N`: index must be ≤ current array length
+- `ts remove` without `--index`: at least one value required
+- `ts remove -i N` with `--index`: no values expected (index is the selector)
+- `ts remove -i N`: index must be < current array length
+
+### Implementation steps
+
+#### Step 1: Add `add_items` and `remove_items` to `edit.rs`
+
+New functions in `src/ts_cmd/edit.rs`:
+
+- **`add_items(doc, key, values: &[String], index: Option<usize>)`** — like
+  `append_field` but takes a `Vec<String>` directly (no string parsing), with
+  optional insert-at-index. Deduplicates on append; for insert, adds at position
+  without dedup (user explicitly chose the position).
+
+- **`remove_items_by_value(doc, key, values: &[String])`** — like
+  `remove_from_field` but takes `Vec<String>` directly. If array becomes empty,
+  removes the field (existing behavior).
+
+- **`remove_item_by_index(doc, key, index: usize)`** — removes the item at
+  the given index. If array becomes empty, removes the field.
+
+- **`set_field_from_strings(doc, key, values: &[String], kind)`** — for `ts set`.
+  Scalars: `values[0]`. Arrays: builds array from all values. Replaces
+  `set_field(doc, key, value_str, kind)` which parsed from a single string.
+
+Keep `parse_array_value()` and `parse_scalar_value()` as internal helpers but
+the public API takes `&[String]` — the shell already did the splitting.
+
+#### Step 2: Add `add_value` and `remove_value` to `ts_cmd/`
+
+**New file:** `src/ts_cmd/add.rs`
+
+```rust
+pub fn add_value(
+    project_root: &Path,
+    package: Option<&str>,
+    key: &str,
+    values: &[String],
+    index: Option<usize>,
+    tspec: Option<&str>,
+) -> Result<()>
+```
+
+- Validate key is an array field (error if scalar)
+- Read, parse DocumentMut, call `edit::add_items()`, write back
+
+**New file:** `src/ts_cmd/remove.rs`
+
+```rust
+pub fn remove_value(
+    project_root: &Path,
+    package: Option<&str>,
+    key: &str,
+    values: &[String],
+    index: Option<usize>,
+    tspec: Option<&str>,
+) -> Result<()>
+```
+
+- Validate key is an array field (error if scalar)
+- If `index` is Some: call `edit::remove_item_by_index()`
+- If `index` is None and values non-empty: call `edit::remove_items_by_value()`
+- If neither: error
+
+#### Step 3: Rewrite `set_value` to accept `&[String]`
+
+**File:** `src/ts_cmd/set.rs`
+
+- Change signature: `value: &str` → `values: &[String]`
+- For scalar fields: validate `values.len() == 1`, then use `values[0]`
+- For array fields: pass all values to `edit::set_field_from_strings()`
+- Remove `parse_assignment()` from `src/cmd/ts.rs`
+
+#### Step 4: Wire up `Add` and `Remove` in CLI
+
+**File:** `src/cmd/ts.rs`
+
+- Add `Add` and `Remove` variants to `TsCommands`
+- Add match arms in `TsCmd::execute()`
+- Remove `parse_assignment()` (no longer needed)
+- Update `Set` variant: `assignment: String` → `key: String` + `value: Vec<String>`
+
+**File:** `src/ts_cmd/mod.rs`
+
+- Add `mod add; mod remove;`
+- Add `pub use add::add_value; pub use remove::remove_value;`
+- Remove `pub use edit::SetOp;` (no longer needed in public API)
+
+#### Step 5: Clean up dead code
+
+- Remove `SetOp` enum from `edit.rs` (or make it `pub(crate)`)
+- Remove `append_field()`, `remove_from_field()`, `parse_array_value()`
+  if no longer called (the new functions take `&[String]` instead of
+  parsing bracket syntax)
+- Remove `parse_assignment()` from `cmd/ts.rs`
+
+#### Step 6: Update tests
+
+- Rewrite `set.rs` tests to use the new `values: &[String]` signature
+- Add `add.rs` tests: append, insert-at-index, dedup, scalar-rejection
+- Add `remove.rs` tests: by-value, by-index, empty-removes-field,
+  scalar-rejection, out-of-bounds index error
+- Add `edit.rs` unit tests for the new functions
+- Remove tests for old bracket-syntax parsing (dead code)
+
+#### Step 7: Update README
+
+- Update `ts set` examples to new syntax
+- Document `ts add` and `ts remove` commands
+
+### Files to modify
+
+| File | Change |
+|---|---|
+| `src/ts_cmd/edit.rs` | Add `add_items`, `remove_items_by_value`, `remove_item_by_index`, `set_field_from_strings`; clean up old API |
+| `src/ts_cmd/set.rs` | Rewrite to accept `&[String]` |
+| `src/ts_cmd/add.rs` | **NEW** — `add_value` |
+| `src/ts_cmd/remove.rs` | **NEW** — `remove_value` |
+| `src/ts_cmd/mod.rs` | Register new modules, update exports |
+| `src/cmd/ts.rs` | Add `Add`/`Remove` variants, rewrite `Set`, remove `parse_assignment()` |
+| `README.md` | Update syntax examples |
+
+### Backward compatibility
+
+The old single-arg `key=value` / `key+=value` / `key-=value` syntax is removed.
+This is a breaking change, but the old syntax is the problem — keeping it as a
+fallback perpetuates the quoting issues.
+
+### Verification
+
+```bash
+tspec test -p tspec
+tspec clippy
+tspec fmt --check
+
+# Manual smoke tests:
+tspec ts set rustc.lto true
+tspec ts set cargo.profile release
+tspec ts set linker.args -static -nostdlib
+tspec ts add linker.args -Wl,--gc-sections
+tspec ts add -i 0 linker.args -nostartfiles
+tspec ts remove linker.args -static
+tspec ts remove -i 0 linker.args
+tspec ts unset linker.args
+```

     1→//! toml_edit helpers for surgical tspec editing that preserves comments/formatting.
     2→
     3→use anyhow::{Result, bail};
     4→use toml_edit::{Array, DocumentMut, Item, Value};
     5→
     6→/// Whether a field holds a scalar or an array.
     7→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
     8→pub enum FieldKind {
     9→    Scalar,
    10→    Array,
    11→}
    12→
    13→/// The set operation to perform.
    14→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
    15→pub enum SetOp {
    16→    /// Replace the field (`=`)
    17→    Replace,
    18→    /// Append to an array (`+=`)
    19→    Append,
    20→    /// Remove from an array (`-=`)
    21→    Remove,
    22→}
    23→
    24→/// Registry entry: (dotted key path, kind).
    25→const FIELD_REGISTRY: &[(&str, FieldKind)] = &[
    26→    ("panic", FieldKind::Scalar),
    27→    ("strip", FieldKind::Scalar),
    28→    ("cargo.profile", FieldKind::Scalar),
    29→    ("cargo.target_triple", FieldKind::Scalar),
    30→    ("cargo.target_json", FieldKind::Scalar),
    31→    ("cargo.target_dir", FieldKind::Scalar),
    32→    ("cargo.unstable", FieldKind::Array),
    33→    ("rustc.opt_level", FieldKind::Scalar),
    34→    ("rustc.panic", FieldKind::Scalar),
    35→    ("rustc.lto", FieldKind::Scalar),
    36→    ("rustc.codegen_units", FieldKind::Scalar),
    37→    ("rustc.build_std", FieldKind::Array),
    38→    ("rustc.flags", FieldKind::Array),
    39→    ("linker.args", FieldKind::Array),
    40→];
    41→
    42→/// Validate that a key is in the registry and return its kind.
    43→pub fn validate_key(key: &str) -> Result<FieldKind> {
    44→    for &(k, kind) in FIELD_REGISTRY {
    45→        if k == key {
    46→            return Ok(kind);
    47→        }
    48→    }
    49→    let valid_keys: Vec<&str> = FIELD_REGISTRY.iter().map(|(k, _)| *k).collect();
    50→    bail!(
    51→        "unknown key: {} (valid keys: {})",
    52→        key,
    53→        valid_keys.join(", ")
    54→    )
    55→}
    56→
    57→/// Validate a value for enum-constrained fields.
    58→/// For unconstrained fields (strings, arrays), accepts anything.
    59→pub fn validate_value(key: &str, value: &str) -> Result<()> {
    60→    match key {
    61→        "panic" => match value {
    62→            "unwind" | "abort" | "immediate-abort" => Ok(()),
    63→            _ => bail!(
    64→                "invalid panic mode: {} (expected: unwind, abort, immediate-abort)",
    65→                value
    66→            ),
    67→        },
    68→        "strip" => match value {
    69→            "none" | "debuginfo" | "symbols" => Ok(()),
    70→            _ => bail!(
    71→                "invalid strip mode: {} (expected: none, debuginfo, symbols)",
    72→                value
    73→            ),
    74→        },
    75→        "cargo.profile" => match value {
    76→            "debug" | "release" => Ok(()),
    77→            _ => bail!("invalid profile: {} (expected: debug, release)", value),
    78→        },
    79→        "rustc.opt_level" => match value {
    80→            "0" | "1" | "2" | "3" | "s" | "z" => Ok(()),
    81→            _ => bail!("invalid opt-level: {} (expected: 0, 1, 2, 3, s, z)", value),
    82→        },
    83→        "rustc.panic" => match value {
    84→            "abort" | "unwind" | "immediate-abort" => Ok(()),
    85→            _ => bail!(
    86→                "invalid panic strategy: {} (expected: abort, unwind, immediate-abort)",
    87→                value
    88→            ),
    89→        },
    90→        "rustc.lto" => match value {
    91→            "true" | "false" | "yes" | "no" | "1" | "0" => Ok(()),
    92→            _ => bail!("invalid boolean: {} (expected: true/false)", value),
    93→        },
    94→        "rustc.codegen_units" => {
    95→            value.parse::<u32>().map_err(|_| {
    96→                anyhow::anyhow!("invalid codegen_units: {} (expected integer)", value)
    97→            })?;
    98→            Ok(())
    99→        }
   100→        _ => Ok(()),
   101→    }
   102→}
   103→
   104→/// Parse a key into (optional table, field).
   105→/// "panic" -> (None, "panic")
   106→/// "rustc.lto" -> (Some("rustc"), "lto")
   107→fn parse_key(key: &str) -> (Option<&str>, &str) {
   108→    match key.split_once('.') {
   109→        Some((table, field)) => (Some(table), field),
   110→        None => (None, key),
   111→    }
   112→}
   113→
   114→/// Parse a value string into a toml_edit Value.
   115→/// Booleans -> bool, integers -> i64, everything else -> string.
   116→fn parse_scalar_value(key: &str, raw: &str) -> Value {
   117→    // For rustc.lto, always parse as boolean
   118→    if key == "rustc.lto" {
   119→        return match raw {
   120→            "true" | "yes" | "1" => Value::from(true),
   121→            _ => Value::from(false),
   122→        };
   123→    }
   124→
   125→    // For rustc.codegen_units, always parse as integer
   126→    if key == "rustc.codegen_units"
   127→        && let Ok(n) = raw.parse::<i64>()
   128→    {
   129→        return Value::from(n);
   130→    }
   131→
   132→    // For rustc.opt_level, keep as string (since "0","1",etc. are enum variants)
   133→    Value::from(raw)
   134→}
   135→
   136→/// Parse an array value from a string.
   137→/// With brackets: `["a","b"]` — parsed as TOML inline array (multiple items).
   138→///   If TOML parse fails, falls back to comma-splitting bare strings: `[a,b]` → `["a","b"]`.
   139→/// Without brackets: treated as a single item (commas are literal).
   140→fn parse_array_value(raw: &str) -> Result<Array> {
   141→    let raw = raw.trim();
   142→
   143→    // Bracket syntax: try TOML first, fall back to bare comma-split
   144→    if raw.starts_with('[') && raw.ends_with(']') {
   145→        let toml_str = format!("x = {}", raw);
   146→        if let Ok(doc) = toml_str.parse::<DocumentMut>()
   147→            && let Some(Item::Value(Value::Array(arr))) = doc.get("x")
   148→        {
   149→            return Ok(arr.clone());
   150→        }
   151→
   152→        // Fallback: treat bracket contents as comma-separated bare strings
   153→        let inner = &raw[1..raw.len() - 1];
   154→        let mut arr = Array::new();
   155→        for item in inner.split(',') {
   156→            let item = item.trim().trim_matches('"').trim_matches('\'');
   157→            if !item.is_empty() {
   158→                arr.push(item);
   159→            }
   160→        }
   161→        return Ok(arr);
   162→    }
   163→
   164→    // No brackets: single item (commas are literal, e.g. "-Wl,--gc-sections")
   165→    let mut arr = Array::new();
   166→    let item = raw.trim_matches('"').trim_matches('\'');
   167→    if !item.is_empty() {
   168→        arr.push(item);
   169→    }
   170→    Ok(arr)
   171→}
   172→
   173→/// Set a field in a toml_edit document.
   174→pub fn set_field(doc: &mut DocumentMut, key: &str, value: &str, kind: FieldKind) -> Result<()> {
   175→    let (table_name, field) = parse_key(key);
   176→
   177→    match kind {
   178→        FieldKind::Scalar => {
   179→            let val = parse_scalar_value(key, value);
   180→            match table_name {
   181→                Some(table) => {
   182→                    // Ensure table exists
   183→                    if doc.get(table).is_none() {
   184→                        doc[table] = toml_edit::Item::Table(toml_edit::Table::new());
   185→                    }
   186→                    doc[table][field] = toml_edit::Item::Value(val);
   187→                }
   188→                None => {
   189→                    doc[field] = toml_edit::Item::Value(val);
   190→                }
   191→            }
   192→        }
   193→        FieldKind::Array => {
   194→            let arr = parse_array_value(value)?;
   195→            match table_name {
   196→                Some(table) => {
   197→                    if doc.get(table).is_none() {
   198→                        doc[table] = toml_edit::Item::Table(toml_edit::Table::new());
   199→                    }
   200→                    doc[table][field] = toml_edit::Item::Value(Value::Array(arr));
   201→                }
   202→                None => {
   203→                    doc[field] = toml_edit::Item::Value(Value::Array(arr));
   204→                }
   205→            }
   206→        }
   207→    }
   208→
   209→    Ok(())
   210→}
   211→
   212→/// Get the existing array for a field, or an empty array if it doesn't exist.
   213→fn get_existing_array(doc: &DocumentMut, key: &str) -> Array {
   214→    let (table_name, field) = parse_key(key);
   215→    match table_name {
   216→        Some(table) => doc
   217→            .get(table)
   218→            .and_then(|t| t.get(field))
   219→            .and_then(|v| v.as_array())
   220→            .cloned()
   221→            .unwrap_or_default(),
   222→        None => doc
   223→            .get(field)
   224→            .and_then(|v| v.as_array())
   225→            .cloned()
   226→            .unwrap_or_default(),
   227→    }
   228→}
   229→
   230→/// Append values to an array field. Creates the array if it doesn't exist.
   231→/// Skips values that are already present (dedup).
   232→pub fn append_field(doc: &mut DocumentMut, key: &str, value: &str) -> Result<()> {
   233→    let (table_name, field) = parse_key(key);
   234→    let mut arr = get_existing_array(doc, key);
   235→    let new_items = parse_array_value(value)?;
   236→
   237→    // Collect existing string values for dedup
   238→    let existing: Vec<String> = arr
   239→        .iter()
   240→        .filter_map(|v| v.as_str().map(|s| s.to_string()))
   241→        .collect();
   242→
   243→    for item in new_items.iter() {
   244→        if let Some(s) = item.as_str()
   245→            && !existing.contains(&s.to_string())
   246→        {
   247→            arr.push(s);
   248→        }
   249→    }
   250→
   251→    match table_name {
   252→        Some(table) => {
   253→            if doc.get(table).is_none() {
   254→                doc[table] = toml_edit::Item::Table(toml_edit::Table::new());
   255→            }
   256→            doc[table][field] = toml_edit::Item::Value(Value::Array(arr));
   257→        }
   258→        None => {
   259→            doc[field] = toml_edit::Item::Value(Value::Array(arr));
   260→        }
   261→    }
   262→
   263→    Ok(())
   264→}
   265→
   266→/// Remove values from an array field. If the array becomes empty, removes the field.
   267→/// If the containing table becomes empty, removes the table too.
   268→pub fn remove_from_field(doc: &mut DocumentMut, key: &str, value: &str) -> Result<()> {
   269→    let (table_name, field) = parse_key(key);
   270→    let arr = get_existing_array(doc, key);
   271→    let to_remove = parse_array_value(value)?;
   272→
   273→    let remove_strs: Vec<String> = to_remove
   274→        .iter()
   275→        .filter_map(|v| v.as_str().map(|s| s.to_string()))
   276→        .collect();
   277→
   278→    let mut new_arr = Array::new();
   279→    for item in arr.iter() {
   280→        if let Some(s) = item.as_str()
   281→            && !remove_strs.contains(&s.to_string())
   282→        {
   283→            new_arr.push(s);
   284→        }
   285→    }
   286→
   287→    if new_arr.is_empty() {
   288→        // Remove the field entirely
   289→        unset_field(doc, key)?;
   290→    } else {
   291→        match table_name {
   292→            Some(table) => {
   293→                if doc.get(table).is_none() {
   294→                    doc[table] = toml_edit::Item::Table(toml_edit::Table::new());
   295→                }
   296→                doc[table][field] = toml_edit::Item::Value(Value::Array(new_arr));
   297→            }
   298→            None => {
   299→                doc[field] = toml_edit::Item::Value(Value::Array(new_arr));
   300→            }
   301→        }
   302→    }
   303→
   304→    Ok(())
   305→}
   306→
   307→/// Remove a field from a toml_edit document.
   308→/// If the containing table becomes empty, remove it too.
   309→pub fn unset_field(doc: &mut DocumentMut, key: &str) -> Result<()> {
   310→    let (table_name, field) = parse_key(key);
   311→
   312→    match table_name {
   313→        Some(table) => {
   314→            if let Some(Item::Table(tbl)) = doc.get_mut(table) {
   315→                tbl.remove(field);
   316→                // If table is now empty, remove it
   317→                if tbl.is_empty() {
   318→                    doc.remove(table);
   319→                }
   320→            }
   321→        }
   322→        None => {
   323→            doc.remove(field);
   324→        }
   325→    }
   326→
   327→    Ok(())
   328→}
   329→
   330→#[cfg(test)]
   331→mod tests {
   332→    use super::*;
   333→
   334→    #[test]
   335→    fn validate_key_valid_scalar() {
   336→        assert_eq!(validate_key("panic").unwrap(), FieldKind::Scalar);
   337→        assert_eq!(validate_key("rustc.lto").unwrap(), FieldKind::Scalar);
   338→        assert_eq!(validate_key("cargo.profile").unwrap(), FieldKind::Scalar);
   339→    }
   340→
   341→    #[test]
   342→    fn validate_key_valid_array() {
   343→        assert_eq!(validate_key("rustc.build_std").unwrap(), FieldKind::Array);
   344→        assert_eq!(validate_key("linker.args").unwrap(), FieldKind::Array);
   345→        assert_eq!(validate_key("cargo.unstable").unwrap(), FieldKind::Array);
   346→        assert_eq!(validate_key("rustc.flags").unwrap(), FieldKind::Array);
   347→    }
   348→
   349→    #[test]
   350→    fn validate_key_unknown() {
   351→        let err = validate_key("nonexistent").unwrap_err();
   352→        assert!(err.to_string().contains("unknown key"));
   353→    }

  "content": "//! toml_edit helpers for surgical tspec editing that preserves comments/formatting.\n\nuse anyhow::{Result, bail};\nuse toml_edit::{Array, DocumentMut, Item, Value};\n\n/// Whether a field holds a scalar or an array.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub enum FieldKind {\n    Scalar,\n    Array,\n}\n\n/// Registry entry: (dotted key path, kind).\nconst FIELD_REGISTRY: &[(&str, FieldKind)] = &[\n    (\"panic\", FieldKind::Scalar),\n    (\"strip\", FieldKind::Scalar),\n    (\"cargo.profile\", FieldKind::Scalar),\n    (\"cargo.target_triple\", FieldKind::Scalar),\n    (\"cargo.target_json\", FieldKind::Scalar),\n    (\"cargo.target_dir\", FieldKind::Scalar),\n    (\"cargo.unstable\", FieldKind::Array),\n    (\"rustc.opt_level\", FieldKind::Scalar),\n    (\"rustc.panic\", FieldKind::Scalar),\n    (\"rustc.lto\", FieldKind::Scalar),\n    (\"rustc.codegen_units\", FieldKind::Scalar),\n    (\"rustc.build_std\", FieldKind::Array),\n    (\"rustc.flags\", FieldKind::Array),\n    (\"linker.args\", FieldKind::Array),\n];\n\n/// Validate that a key is in the registry and return its kind.\npub fn validate_key(key: &str) -> Result<FieldKind> {\n    for &(k, kind) in FIELD_REGISTRY {\n        if k == key {\n            return Ok(kind);\n        }\n    }\n    let valid_keys: Vec<&str> = FIELD_REGISTRY.iter().map(|(k, _)| *k).collect();\n    bail!(\n        \"unknown key: {} (valid keys: {})\",\n        key,\n        valid_keys.join(\", \")\n    )\n}\n\n/// Validate a value for enum-constrained fields.\n/// For unconstrained fields (strings, arrays), accepts anything.\npub fn validate_value(key: &str, value: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    match key {\n        \"panic\" => match value {\n            \"unwind\" | \"abort\" | \"immediate-abort\" => Ok(()),\n            _ => bail!(\n                \"invalid panic mode: {} (expected: unwind, abort, immediate-abort)\",\n                value\n            ),\n        },\n        \"strip\" => match value {\n            \"none\" | \"debuginfo\" | \"symbols\" => Ok(()),\n            _ => bail!(\n                \"invalid strip mode: {} (expected: none, debuginfo, symbols)\",\n                value\n            ),\n        },\n        \"cargo.profile\" => match value {\n            \"debug\" | \"release\" => Ok(()),\n            _ => bail!(\"invalid profile: {} (expected: debug, release)\", value),\n        },\n        \"rustc.opt_level\" => match value {\n            \"0\" | \"1\" | \"2\" | \"3\" | \"s\" | \"z\" => Ok(()),\n            _ => bail!(\"invalid opt-level: {} (expected: 0, 1, 2, 3, s, z)\", value),\n        },\n        \"rustc.panic\" => match value {\n            \"abort\" | \"unwind\" | \"immediate-abort\" => Ok(()),\n            _ => bail!(\n                \"invalid panic strategy: {} (expected: abort, unwind, immediate-abort)\",\n                value\n            ),\n        },\n        \"rustc.lto\" => match value {\n            \"true\" | \"false\" | \"yes\" | \"no\" | \"1\" | \"0\" => Ok(()),\n            _ => bail!(\"invalid boolean: {} (expected: true/false)\", value),\n        },\n        \"rustc.codegen_units\" => {\n            value.parse::<u32>().map_err(|_| {\n                anyhow::anyhow!(\"invalid codegen_units: {} (expected integer)\", value)\n            })?;\n            Ok(())\n        }\n        _ => Ok(()),\n    }\n}\n\n/// Parse a key into (optional table, field).\n/// \"panic\" -> (None, \"panic\")\n/// \"rustc.lto\" -> (Some(\"rustc\"), \"lto\")\nfn parse_key(key: &str) -> (Option<&str>, &str) {\n    match key.split_once('.') {\n        Some((table, field)) => (Some(table), field),\n        None => (None, key),\n    }\n}\n\n/// Parse a value string into a toml_edit Value.\n/// Booleans -> bool, integers -> i64, everything else -> string.\nfn parse_scalar_value(key: &str, raw: &str) -> Value {\n    // For rustc.lto, always parse as boolean\n    if key == \"rustc.lto\" {\n        return match raw {\n            \"true\" | \"yes\" | \"1\" => Value::from(true),\n            _ => Value::from(false),\n        };\n    }\n\n    // For rustc.codegen_units, always parse as integer\n    if key == \"rustc.codegen_units\"\n        && let Ok(n) = raw.parse::<i64>()\n    {\n        return Value::from(n);\n    }\n\n    // For rustc.opt_level, keep as string (since \"0\",\"1\",etc. are enum variants)\n    Value::from(raw)\n}\n\n/// Get the existing array for a field, or an empty array if it doesn't exist.\nfn get_existing_array(doc: &DocumentMut, key: &str) -> Array {\n    let (table_name, field) = parse_key(key);\n    match table_name {\n        Some(table) => doc\n            .get(table)\n            .and_then(|t| t.get(field))\n            .and_then(|v| v.as_array())\n            .cloned()\n            .unwrap_or_default(),\n        None => doc\n            .get(field)\n            .and_then(|v| v.as_array())\n            .cloned()\n            .unwrap_or_default(),\n    }\n}\n\n/// Set an array value into the document at the given key path.\nfn set_array_in_doc(doc: &mut DocumentMut, key: &str, arr: Array) {\n    let (table_name, field) = parse_key(key);\n    match table_name {\n        Some(table) => {\n            ensure_table(doc, table);\n            doc[table][field] = Item::Value(Value::Array(arr));\n        }\n        None => {\n            doc[field] = Item::Value(Value::Array(arr));\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Ensure a table exists in the document.\nfn ensure_table(doc: &mut DocumentMut, table: &str) {\n    if doc.get(table).is_none() {\n        doc[table] = Item::Table(toml_edit::Table::new());\n    }\n}\n\n// === Public API: takes &[String] from shell args, no string parsing ===\n\n/// Set a field from string args. Scalars take `values[0]`, arrays take all values.\npub fn set_field(doc: &mut DocumentMut, key: &str, values: &[String], kind: FieldKind) -> Result<()> {\n    let (table_name, field) = parse_key(key);\n\n    match kind {\n        FieldKind::Scalar => {\n            if values.len() != 1 {\n                bail!(\n                    \"scalar field '{}' requires exactly one value, got {}\",\n                    key,\n                    values.len()\n                );\n            }\n            let val = parse_scalar_value(key, &values[0]);\n            match table_name {\n                Some(table) => {\n                    ensure_table(doc, table);\n                    doc[table][field] = Item::Value(val);\n                }\n                None => {\n                    doc[field] = Item::Value(val);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        FieldKind::Array => {\n            let mut arr = Array::new();\n            for v in values {\n                arr.push(v.as_str());\n            }\n            set_array_in_doc(doc, key, arr);\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Add items to an array field. Appends by default, or inserts at `index`.\n/// Deduplicates on append; insert adds at position without dedup.\npub fn add_items(\n    doc: &mut DocumentMut,\n    key: &str,\n    values: &[String],\n    index: Option<usize>,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    let mut arr = get_existing_array(doc, key);\n\n    match index {\n        Some(idx) => {\n            if idx > arr.len() {\n                bail!(\n                    \"index {} out of bounds for array '{}' with {} elements\",\n                    idx,\n                    key,\n                    arr.len()\n                );\n            }\n            // Insert at position (no dedup — user explicitly chose position)\n            for (offset, v) in values.iter().enumerate() {\n                arr.insert(idx + offset, v.as_str());\n            }\n        }\n        None => {\n            // Append with dedup\n            let existing: Vec<String> = arr\n                .iter()\n                .filter_map(|v| v.as_str().map(|s| s.to_string()))\n                .collect();\n            for v in values {\n                if !existing.contains(v) {\n                    arr.push(v.as_str());\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    set_array_in_doc(doc, key, arr);\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Remove items from an array field by value.\n/// If the array becomes empty, removes the field (and empty table).\npub fn remove_items_by_value(doc: &mut DocumentMut, key: &str, values: &[String]) -> Result<()> {\n    let arr = get_existing_array(doc, key);\n\n    let mut new_arr = Array::new();\n    for item in arr.iter() {\n        if let Some(s) = item.as_str() {\n            if !values.iter().any(|v| v == s) {\n                new_arr.push(s);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    if new_arr.is_empty() {\n        unset_field(doc, key)?;\n    } else {\n        set_array_in_doc(doc, key, new_arr);\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Remove an item from an array field by index.\n/// If the array becomes empty, removes the field (and empty table).\npub fn remove_item_by_index(doc: &mut DocumentMut, key: &str, index: usize) -> Result<()> {\n    let mut arr = get_existing_array(doc, key);\n\n    if index >= arr.len() {\n        bail!(\n            \"index {} out of bounds for array '{}' with {} elements\",\n            index,\n            key,\n            arr.len()\n        );\n    }\n\n    arr.remove(index);\n\n    if arr.is_empty() {\n        unset_field(doc, key)?;\n    } else {\n        set_array_in_doc(doc, key, arr);\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Remove a field from a toml_edit document.\n/// If the containing table becomes empty, remove it too.\npub fn unset_field(doc: &mut DocumentMut, key: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    let (table_name, field) = parse_key(key);\n\n    match table_name {\n        Some(table) => {\n            if let Some(Item::Table(tbl)) = doc.get_mut(table) {\n                tbl.remove(field);\n                // If table is now empty, remove it\n                if tbl.is_empty() {\n                    doc.remove(table);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        None => {\n            doc.remove(field);\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n\n    // Helper to make a Vec<String> from string literals\n    fn vs(items: &[&str]) -> Vec<String> {\n        items.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect()\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn validate_key_valid_scalar() {\n        assert_eq!(validate_key(\"panic\").unwrap(), FieldKind::Scalar);\n        assert_eq!(validate_key(\"rustc.lto\").unwrap(), FieldKind::Scalar);\n        assert_eq!(validate_key(\"cargo.profile\").unwrap(), FieldKind::Scalar);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn validate_key_valid_array() {\n        assert_eq!(validate_key(\"rustc.build_std\").unwrap(), FieldKind::Array);\n        assert_eq!(validate_key(\"linker.args\").unwrap(), FieldKind::Array);\n        assert_eq!(validate_key(\"cargo.unstable\").unwrap(), FieldKind::Array);\n        assert_eq!(validate_key(\"rustc.flags\").unwrap(), FieldKind::Array);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn validate_key_unknown() {\n        let err = validate_key(\"nonexistent\").unwrap_err();\n        assert!(err.to_string().contains(\"unknown key\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn validate_value_panic() {\n        assert!(validate_value(\"panic\", \"abort\").is_ok());\n        assert!(validate_value(\"panic\", \"unwind\").is_ok());\n        assert!(validate_value(\"panic\", \"immediate-abort\").is_ok());\n        assert!(validate_value(\"panic\", \"invalid\").is_err());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn validate_value_strip() {\n        assert!(validate_value(\"strip\", \"none\").is_ok());\n        assert!(validate_value(\"strip\", \"debuginfo\").is_ok());\n        assert!(validate_value(\"strip\", \"symbols\").is_ok());\n        assert!(validate_value(\"strip\", \"invalid\").is_err());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn validate_value_profile() {\n        assert!(validate_value(\"cargo.profile\", \"debug\").is_ok());\n        assert!(validate_value(\"cargo.profile\", \"release\").is_ok());\n        assert!(validate_value(\"cargo.profile\", \"invalid\").is_err());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn validate_value_lto() {\n        assert!(validate_value(\"rustc.lto\", \"true\").is_ok());\n        assert!(validate_value(\"rustc.lto\", \"false\").is_ok());\n        assert!(validate_value(\"rustc.lto\", \"invalid\").is_err());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn validate_value_codegen_units() {\n        assert!(validate_value(\"rustc.codegen_units\", \"1\").is_ok());\n        assert!(validate_value(\"rustc.codegen_units\", \"16\").is_ok());\n        assert!(validate_value(\"rustc.codegen_units\", \"abc\").is_err());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn validate_value_unconstrained() {\n        assert!(validate_value(\"cargo.target_triple\", \"anything\").is_ok());\n        assert!(validate_value(\"cargo.target_dir\", \"anything\").is_ok());\n        assert!(validate_value(\"linker.args\", \"anything\").is_ok());\n    }\n\n    // --- set_field tests ---\n\n    #[test]\n    fn set_toplevel_scalar() {\n        let mut doc = \"\".parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        set_field(&mut doc, \"panic\", &vs(&[\"abort\"]), FieldKind::Scalar).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(doc[\"panic\"].as_str(), Some(\"abort\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn set_nested_scalar() {\n        let mut doc = \"\".parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        set_field(&mut doc, \"rustc.lto\", &vs(&[\"true\"]), FieldKind::Scalar).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(doc[\"rustc\"][\"lto\"].as_bool(), Some(true));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn set_nested_scalar_creates_table() {\n        let mut doc = \"\".parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        set_field(&mut doc, \"cargo.profile\", &vs(&[\"release\"]), FieldKind::Scalar).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(doc[\"cargo\"][\"profile\"].as_str(), Some(\"release\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn set_array_multiple_values() {\n        let mut doc = \"\".parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        set_field(\n            &mut doc,\n            \"linker.args\",\n            &vs(&[\"-static\", \"-nostdlib\"]),\n            FieldKind::Array,\n        )\n        .unwrap();\n        let arr = doc[\"linker\"][\"args\"].as_array().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(arr.len(), 2);\n        assert_eq!(arr.get(0).unwrap().as_str(), Some(\"-static\"));\n        assert_eq!(arr.get(1).unwrap().as_str(), Some(\"-nostdlib\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn set_array_single_value() {\n        let mut doc = \"\".parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        set_field(&mut doc, \"linker.args\", &vs(&[\"-static\"]), FieldKind::Array).unwrap();\n        let arr = doc[\"linker\"][\"args\"].as_array().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(arr.len(), 1);\n        assert_eq!(arr.get(0).unwrap().as_str(), Some(\"-static\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn set_codegen_units_as_integer() {\n        let mut doc = \"\".parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        set_field(&mut doc, \"rustc.codegen_units\", &vs(&[\"1\"]), FieldKind::Scalar).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(doc[\"rustc\"][\"codegen_units\"].as_integer(), Some(1));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn set_preserves_existing_content() {\n        let input = \"# My comment\\npanic = \\\"unwind\\\"\\n\";\n        let mut doc = input.parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        set_field(&mut doc, \"strip\", &vs(&[\"symbols\"]), FieldKind::Scalar).unwrap();\n        let output = doc.to_string();\n        assert!(output.contains(\"# My comment\"));\n        assert!(output.contains(\"strip = \\\"symbols\\\"\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn set_scalar_rejects_multiple_values() {\n        let mut doc = \"\".parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        let err = set_field(&mut doc, \"panic\", &vs(&[\"abort\", \"unwind\"]), FieldKind::Scalar);\n        assert!(err.is_err());\n        assert!(err.unwrap_err().to_string().contains(\"exactly one value\"));\n    }\n\n    // --- unset_field tests ---\n\n    #[test]\n    fn unset_toplevel() {\n        let input = \"panic = \\\"abort\\\"\\nstrip = \\\"symbols\\\"\\n\";\n        let mut doc = input.parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        unset_field(&mut doc, \"panic\").unwrap();\n        assert!(doc.get(\"panic\").is_none());\n        assert!(doc.get(\"strip\").is_some());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn unset_nested() {\n        let input = \"[rustc]\\nlto = true\\nopt_level = \\\"3\\\"\\n\";\n        let mut doc = input.parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        unset_field(&mut doc, \"rustc.lto\").unwrap();\n        assert!(doc[\"rustc\"].get(\"lto\").is_none());\n        assert!(doc[\"rustc\"].get(\"opt_level\").is_some());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn unset_removes_empty_table() {\n        let input = \"[rustc]\\nlto = true\\n\";\n        let mut doc = input.parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        unset_field(&mut doc, \"rustc.lto\").unwrap();\n        assert!(doc.get(\"rustc\").is_none());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn unset_nonexistent_is_ok() {\n        let mut doc = \"\".parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        unset_field(&mut doc, \"panic\").unwrap();\n        unset_field(&mut doc, \"rustc.lto\").unwrap();\n    }\n\n    // --- add_items tests ---\n\n    #[test]\n    fn add_append_to_empty() {\n        let mut doc = \"\".parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        add_items(&mut doc, \"linker.args\", &vs(&[\"-static\"]), None).unwrap();\n        let arr = doc[\"linker\"][\"args\"].as_array().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(arr.len(), 1);\n        assert_eq!(arr.get(0).unwrap().as_str(), Some(\"-static\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn add_append_to_existing() {\n        let input = \"[linker]\\nargs = [\\\"-static\\\"]\\n\";\n        let mut doc = input.parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        add_items(&mut doc, \"linker.args\", &vs(&[\"-nostdlib\"]), None).unwrap();\n        let arr = doc[\"linker\"][\"args\"].as_array().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(arr.len(), 2);\n        assert_eq!(arr.get(0).unwrap().as_str(), Some(\"-static\"));\n        assert_eq!(arr.get(1).unwrap().as_str(), Some(\"-nostdlib\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn add_append_deduplicates() {\n        let input = \"[linker]\\nargs = [\\\"-static\\\"]\\n\";\n        let mut doc = input.parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        add_items(&mut doc, \"linker.args\", &vs(&[\"-static\"]), None).unwrap();\n        let arr = doc[\"linker\"][\"args\"].as_array().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(arr.len(), 1);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn add_append_multiple() {\n        let mut doc = \"\".parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        add_items(\n            &mut doc,\n            \"linker.args\",\n            &vs(&[\"-static\", \"-nostdlib\"]),\n            None,\n        )\n        .unwrap();\n        let arr = doc[\"linker\"][\"args\"].as_array().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(arr.len(), 2);\n        assert_eq!(arr.get(0).unwrap().as_str(), Some(\"-static\"));\n        assert_eq!(arr.get(1).unwrap().as_str(), Some(\"-nostdlib\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn add_insert_at_beginning() {\n        let input = \"[linker]\\nargs = [\\\"-static\\\", \\\"-nostdlib\\\"]\\n\";\n        let mut doc = input.parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        add_items(\n            &mut doc,\n            \"linker.args\",\n            &vs(&[\"-nostartfiles\"]),\n            Some(0),\n        )\n        .unwrap();\n        let arr = doc[\"linker\"][\"args\"].as_array().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(arr.len(), 3);\n        assert_eq!(arr.get(0).unwrap().as_str(), Some(\"-nostartfiles\"));\n        assert_eq!(arr.get(1).unwrap().as_str(), Some(\"-static\"));\n        assert_eq!(arr.get(2).unwrap().as_str(), Some(\"-nostdlib\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn add_insert_at_middle() {\n        let input = \"[linker]\\nargs = [\\\"-static\\\", \\\"-nostdlib\\\"]\\n\";\n        let mut doc = input.parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        add_items(\n            &mut doc,\n            \"linker.args\",\n            &vs(&[\"-Wl,--gc-sections\"]),\n            Some(1),\n        )\n        .unwrap();\n        let arr = doc[\"linker\"][\"args\"].as_array().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(arr.len(), 3);\n        assert_eq!(arr.get(0).unwrap().as_str(), Some(\"-static\"));\n        assert_eq!(arr.get(1).unwrap().as_str(), Some(\"-Wl,--gc-sections\"));\n        assert_eq!(arr.get(2).unwrap().as_str(), Some(\"-nostdlib\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn add_insert_at_end() {\n        let input = \"[linker]\\nargs = [\\\"-static\\\"]\\n\";\n        let mut doc = input.parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        add_items(&mut doc, \"linker.args\", &vs(&[\"-nostdlib\"]), Some(1)).unwrap();\n        let arr = doc[\"linker\"][\"args\"].as_array().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(arr.len(), 2);\n        assert_eq!(arr.get(0).unwrap().as_str(), Some(\"-static\"));\n        assert_eq!(arr.get(1).unwrap().as_str(), Some(\"-nostdlib\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn add_insert_out_of_bounds() {\n        let mut doc = \"\".parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        let err = add_items(&mut doc, \"linker.args\", &vs(&[\"-static\"]), Some(1));\n        assert!(err.is_err());\n        assert!(err.unwrap_err().to_string().contains(\"out of bounds\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn add_insert_does_not_dedup() {\n        let input = \"[linker]\\nargs = [\\\"-static\\\"]\\n\";\n        let mut doc = input.parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        add_items(&mut doc, \"linker.args\", &vs(&[\"-static\"]), Some(0)).unwrap();\n        let arr = doc[\"linker\"][\"args\"].as_array().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(arr.len(), 2);\n    }\n\n    // --- remove_items_by_value tests ---\n\n    #[test]\n    fn remove_one_by_value() {\n        let input = \"[linker]\\nargs = [\\\"-static\\\", \\\"-nostdlib\\\"]\\n\";\n        let mut doc = input.parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        remove_items_by_value(&mut doc, \"linker.args\", &vs(&[\"-nostdlib\"])).unwrap();\n        let arr = doc[\"linker\"][\"args\"].as_array().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(arr.len(), 1);\n        assert_eq!(arr.get(0).unwrap().as_str(), Some(\"-static\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn remove_multiple_by_value() {\n        let input = \"[linker]\\nargs = [\\\"-static\\\", \\\"-nostdlib\\\", \\\"-Wl,--gc-sections\\\"]\\n\";\n        let mut doc = input.parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        remove_items_by_value(\n            &mut doc,\n            \"linker.args\",\n            &vs(&[\"-static\", \"-Wl,--gc-sections\"]),\n        )\n        .unwrap();\n        let arr = doc[\"linker\"][\"args\"].as_array().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(arr.len(), 1);\n        assert_eq!(arr.get(0).unwrap().as_str(), Some(\"-nostdlib\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn remove_last_removes_field_and_table() {\n        let input = \"[linker]\\nargs = [\\\"-static\\\"]\\n\";\n        let mut doc = input.parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        remove_items_by_value(&mut doc, \"linker.args\", &vs(&[\"-static\"])).unwrap();\n        assert!(doc.get(\"linker\").is_none());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn remove_nonexistent_value_is_noop() {\n        let input = \"[linker]\\nargs = [\\\"-static\\\"]\\n\";\n        let mut doc = input.parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        remove_items_by_value(&mut doc, \"linker.args\", &vs(&[\"-nostdlib\"])).unwrap();\n        let arr = doc[\"linker\"][\"args\"].as_array().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(arr.len(), 1);\n    }\n\n    // --- remove_item_by_index tests ---\n\n    #[test]\n    fn remove_by_index_first() {\n        let input = \"[linker]\\nargs = [\\\"-static\\\", \\\"-nostdlib\\\"]\\n\";\n        let mut doc = input.parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        remove_item_by_index(&mut doc, \"linker.args\", 0).unwrap();\n        let arr = doc[\"linker\"][\"args\"].as_array().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(arr.len(), 1);\n        assert_eq!(arr.get(0).unwrap().as_str(), Some(\"-nostdlib\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn remove_by_index_last() {\n        let input = \"[linker]\\nargs = [\\\"-static\\\", \\\"-nostdlib\\\"]\\n\";\n        let mut doc = input.parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        remove_item_by_index(&mut doc, \"linker.args\", 1).unwrap();\n        let arr = doc[\"linker\"][\"args\"].as_array().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(arr.len(), 1);\n        assert_eq!(arr.get(0).unwrap().as_str(), Some(\"-static\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn remove_by_index_last_item_removes_field() {\n        let input = \"[linker]\\nargs = [\\\"-static\\\"]\\n\";\n        let mut doc = input.parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        remove_item_by_index(&mut doc, \"linker.args\", 0).unwrap();\n        assert!(doc.get(\"linker\").is_none());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn remove_by_index_out_of_bounds() {\n        let input = \"[linker]\\nargs = [\\\"-static\\\"]\\n\";\n        let mut doc = input.parse::<DocumentMut>().unwrap();\n        let err = remove_item_by_index(&mut doc, \"linker.args\", 5);\n      1→//! toml_edit helpers for surgical tspec editing that preserves comments/formatting.
     2→
     3→use anyhow::{Result, bail};
     4→use toml_edit::{Array, DocumentMut, Item, Value};
     5→
     6→/// Whether a field holds a scalar or an array.
     7→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
     8→pub enum FieldKind {
     9→    Scalar,
    10→    Array,
    11→}
    12→
    13→/// Registry entry: (dotted key path, kind).
    14→const FIELD_REGISTRY: &[(&str, FieldKind)] = &[
    15→    ("panic", FieldKind::Scalar),
    16→    ("strip", FieldKind::Scalar),
    17→    ("cargo.profile", FieldKind::Scalar),
    18→    ("cargo.target_triple", FieldKind::Scalar),
    19→    ("cargo.target_json", FieldKind::Scalar),
    20→    ("cargo.target_dir", FieldKind::Scalar),
    21→    ("cargo.unstable", FieldKind::Array),
    22→    ("rustc.opt_level", FieldKind::Scalar),
    23→    ("rustc.panic", FieldKind::Scalar),
    24→    ("rustc.lto", FieldKind::Scalar),
    25→    ("rustc.codegen_units", FieldKind::Scalar),
    26→    ("rustc.build_std", FieldKind::Array),
    27→    ("rustc.flags", FieldKind::Array),
    28→    ("linker.args", FieldKind::Array),
    29→];
    30→
    31→/// Validate that a key is in the registry and return its kind.
    32→pub fn validate_key(key: &str) -> Result<FieldKind> {
    33→    for &(k, kind) in FIELD_REGISTRY {
    34→        if k == key {
    35→            return Ok(kind);
    36→        }
    37→    }
    38→    let valid_keys: Vec<&str> = FIELD_REGISTRY.iter().map(|(k, _)| *k).collect();
    39→    bail!(
    40→        "unknown key: {} (valid keys: {})",
    41→        key,
    42→        valid_keys.join(", ")
    43→    )
    44→}
    45→
    46→/// Validate a value for enum-constrained fields.
    47→/// For unconstrained fields (strings, arrays), accepts anything.
    48→pub fn validate_value(key: &str, value: &str) -> Result<()> {
    49→    match key {
    50→        "panic" => match value {
    51→            "unwind" | "abort" | "immediate-abort" => Ok(()),
    52→            _ => bail!(
    53→                "invalid panic mode: {} (expected: unwind, abort, immediate-abort)",
    54→                value
    55→            ),
    56→        },
    57→        "strip" => match value {
    58→            "none" | "debuginfo" | "symbols" => Ok(()),
    59→            _ => bail!(
    60→                "invalid strip mode: {} (expected: none, debuginfo, symbols)",
    61→                value
    62→            ),
    63→        },
    64→        "cargo.profile" => match value {
    65→            "debug" | "release" => Ok(()),
    66→            _ => bail!("invalid profile: {} (expected: debug, release)", value),
    67→        },
    68→        "rustc.opt_level" => match value {
    69→            "0" | "1" | "2" | "3" | "s" | "z" => Ok(()),
    70→            _ => bail!("invalid opt-level: {} (expected: 0, 1, 2, 3, s, z)", value),
    71→        },
    72→        "rustc.panic" => match value {
    73→            "abort" | "unwind" | "immediate-abort" => Ok(()),
    74→            _ => bail!(
    75→                "invalid panic strategy: {} (expected: abort, unwind, immediate-abort)",
    76→                value
    77→            ),
    78→        },
    79→        "rustc.lto" => match value {
    80→            "true" | "false" | "yes" | "no" | "1" | "0" => Ok(()),
    81→            _ => bail!("invalid boolean: {} (expected: true/false)", value),
    82→        },
    83→        "rustc.codegen_units" => {
    84→            value.parse::<u32>().map_err(|_| {
    85→                anyhow::anyhow!("invalid codegen_units: {} (expected integer)", value)
    86→            })?;
    87→            Ok(())
    88→        }
    89→        _ => Ok(()),
    90→    }
    91→}
    92→
    93→/// Parse a key into (optional table, field).
    94→/// "panic" -> (None, "panic")
    95→/// "rustc.lto" -> (Some("rustc"), "lto")
    96→fn parse_key(key: &str) -> (Option<&str>, &str) {
    97→    match key.split_once('.') {
    98→        Some((table, field)) => (Some(table), field),
    99→        None => (None, key),
   100→    }
   101→}
   102→
   103→/// Parse a value string into a toml_edit Value.
   104→/// Booleans -> bool, integers -> i64, everything else -> string.
   105→fn parse_scalar_value(key: &str, raw: &str) -> Value {
   106→    // For rustc.lto, always parse as boolean
   107→    if key == "rustc.lto" {
   108→        return match raw {
   109→            "true" | "yes" | "1" => Value::from(true),
   110→            _ => Value::from(false),
   111→        };
   112→    }
   113→
   114→    // For rustc.codegen_units, always parse as integer
   115→    if key == "rustc.codegen_units"
   116→        && let Ok(n) = raw.parse::<i64>()
   117→    {
   118→        return Value::from(n);
   119→    }
   120→
   121→    // For rustc.opt_level, keep as string (since "0","1",etc. are enum variants)
   122→    Value::from(raw)
   123→}
   124→
   125→/// Get the existing array for a field, or an empty array if it doesn't exist.
   126→fn get_existing_array(doc: &DocumentMut, key: &str) -> Array {
   127→    let (table_name, field) = parse_key(key);
   128→    match table_name {
   129→        Some(table) => doc
   130→            .get(table)
   131→            .and_then(|t| t.get(field))
   132→            .and_then(|v| v.as_array())
   133→            .cloned()
   134→            .unwrap_or_default(),
   135→        None => doc
   136→            .get(field)
   137→            .and_then(|v| v.as_array())
   138→            .cloned()
   139→            .unwrap_or_default(),
   140→    }
   141→}
   142→
   143→/// Set an array value into the document at the given key path.
   144→fn set_array_in_doc(doc: &mut DocumentMut, key: &str, arr: Array) {
   145→    let (table_name, field) = parse_key(key);
   146→    match table_name {
   147→        Some(table) => {
   148→            ensure_table(doc, table);
   149→            doc[table][field] = Item::Value(Value::Array(arr));
   150→        }
   151→        None => {
   152→            doc[field] = Item::Value(Value::Array(arr));
   153→        }
   154→    }
   155→}
   156→
   157→/// Ensure a table exists in the document.
   158→fn ensure_table(doc: &mut DocumentMut, table: &str) {
   159→    if doc.get(table).is_none() {
   160→        doc[table] = Item::Table(toml_edit::Table::new());
   161→    }
   162→}
   163→
   164→// === Public API: takes &[String] from shell args, no string parsing ===
   165→
   166→/// Set a field from string args. Scalars take `values[0]`, arrays take all values.
   167→pub fn set_field(doc: &mut DocumentMut, key: &str, values: &[String], kind: FieldKind) -> Result<()> {
   168→    let (table_name, field) = parse_key(key);
   169→
   170→    match kind {
   171→        FieldKind::Scalar => {
   172→            if values.len() != 1 {
   173→                bail!(
   174→                    "scalar field '{}' requires exactly one value, got {}",
   175→                    key,
   176→                    values.len()
   177→                );
   178→            }
   179→            let val = parse_scalar_value(key, &values[0]);
   180→            match table_name {
   181→                Some(table) => {
   182→                    ensure_table(doc, table);
   183→                    doc[table][field] = Item::Value(val);
   184→                }
   185→                None => {
   186→                    doc[field] = Item::Value(val);
   187→                }
   188→            }
   189→        }
   190→        FieldKind::Array => {
   191→            let mut arr = Array::new();
   192→            for v in values {
   193→                arr.push(v.as_str());
   194→            }
   195→            set_array_in_doc(doc, key, arr);
   196→        }
   197→    }
   198→
   199→    Ok(())
   200→}
   201→
   202→/// Add items to an array field. Appends by default, or inserts at `index`.
   203→/// Deduplicates on append; insert adds at position without dedup.
   204→pub fn add_items(
   205→    doc: &mut DocumentMut,
   206→    key: &str,
   207→    values: &[String],
   208→    index: Option<usize>,
   209→) -> Result<()> {
   210→    let mut arr = get_existing_array(doc, key);
   211→
   212→    match index {
   213→        Some(idx) => {
   214→            if idx > arr.len() {
   215→                bail!(
   216→                    "index {} out of bounds for array '{}' with {} elements",
   217→                    idx,
   218→                    key,
   219→                    arr.len()
   220→                );
   221→            }
   222→            // Insert at position (no dedup — user explicitly chose position)
   223→            for (offset, v) in values.iter().enumerate() {
   224→                arr.insert(idx + offset, v.as_str());
   225→            }
   226→        }
   227→        None => {
   228→            // Append with dedup
   229→            let existing: Vec<String> = arr
   230→                .iter()
   231→                .filter_map(|v| v.as_str().map(|s| s.to_string()))
   232→                .collect();
   233→            for v in values {
   234→                if !existing.contains(v) {
   235→                    arr.push(v.as_str());
   236→                }
   237→            }
   238→        }
   239→    }
   240→
   241→    set_array_in_doc(doc, key, arr);
   242→    Ok(())
   243→}
   244→
   245→/// Remove items from an array field by value.
   246→/// If the array becomes empty, removes the field (and empty table).
   247→pub fn remove_items_by_value(doc: &mut DocumentMut, key: &str, values: &[String]) -> Result<()> {
   248→    let arr = get_existing_array(doc, key);
   249→
   250→    let mut new_arr = Array::new();
   251→    for item in arr.iter() {
   252→        if let Some(s) = item.as_str() {
   253→            if !values.iter().any(|v| v == s) {
   254→                new_arr.push(s);
   255→            }
   256→        }
   257→    }
   258→
   259→    if new_arr.is_empty() {
   260→        unset_field(doc, key)?;
   261→    } else {
   262→        set_array_in_doc(doc, key, new_arr);
   263→    }
   264→
   265→    Ok(())
   266→}
   267→
   268→/// Remove an item from an array field by index.
   269→/// If the array becomes empty, removes the field (and empty table).
   270→pub fn remove_item_by_index(doc: &mut DocumentMut, key: &str, index: usize) -> Result<()> {
   271→    let mut arr = get_existing_array(doc, key);
   272→
   273→    if index >= arr.len() {
   274→        bail!(
   275→            "index {} out of bounds for array '{}' with {} elements",
   276→            index,
   277→            key,
   278→            arr.len()
   279→        );
   280→    }
   281→
   282→    arr.remove(index);
   283→
   284→    if arr.is_empty() {
   285→        unset_field(doc, key)?;
   286→    } else {
   287→        set_array_in_doc(doc, key, arr);
   288→    }
   289→
   290→    Ok(())
   291→}
   292→
   293→/// Remove a field from a toml_edit document.
   294→/// If the containing table becomes empty, remove it too.
   295→pub fn unset_field(doc: &mut DocumentMut, key: &str) -> Result<()> {
   296→    let (table_name, field) = parse_key(key);
   297→
   298→    match table_name {
   299→        Some(table) => {
   300→            if let Some(Item::Table(tbl)) = doc.get_mut(table) {
   301→                tbl.remove(field);
   302→                // If table is now empty, remove it
   303→                if tbl.is_empty() {
   304→                    doc.remove(table);
   305→                }
   306→            }
   307→        }
   308→        None => {
   309→            doc.remove(field);
   310→        }
   311→    }
   312→
   313→    Ok(())
   314→}

     1→//! `tspec ts unset` - Remove a field from a tspec (preserves comments/formatting)
     2→
     3→use anyhow::{Context, Result, bail};
     4→use std::path::Path;
     5→use toml_edit::DocumentMut;
     6→
     7→use super::edit;
     8→use crate::find_paths::{find_tspec, resolve_package_dir};
     9→
    10→/// Remove a field from a tspec
    11→pub fn unset_value(
    12→    project_root: &Path,
    13→    package: Option<&str>,
    14→    key: &str,
    15→    tspec: Option<&str>,
    16→) -> Result<()> {
    17→    let workspace = project_root;
    18→    let package_dir = resolve_package_dir(workspace, package)?;
    19→
    20→    let output_path = match find_tspec(&package_dir, tspec)? {
    21→        Some(path) => path,
    22→        None => bail!("no tspec found to modify"),
    23→    };
    24→
    25→    // Validate the key
    26→    edit::validate_key(key)?;
    27→
    28→    // Read, parse, edit, write
    29→    let content = std::fs::read_to_string(&output_path)
    30→        .with_context(|| format!("failed to read: {}", output_path.display()))?;
    31→
    32→    let mut doc: DocumentMut = content
    33→        .parse()
    34→        .with_context(|| format!("failed to parse: {}", output_path.display()))?;
    35→
    36→    edit::unset_field(&mut doc, key)?;
    37→
    38→    std::fs::write(&output_path, doc.to_string())
    39→        .with_context(|| format!("failed to write: {}", output_path.display()))?;
    40→
    41→    println!(
    42→        "Saved {}",
    43→        output_path
    44→            .strip_prefix(workspace)
    45→            .unwrap_or(&output_path)
    46→            .display()
    47→    );
    48→
    49→    Ok(())
    50→}
    51→
    52→#[cfg(test)]
    53→mod tests {
    54→    use crate::test_constants::SUFFIX;
    55→    use crate::tspec::load_spec;
    56→    use toml_edit::DocumentMut;
    57→
    58→    use super::*;
    59→
    60→    fn unset_in_file(content: &str, key: &str) -> (tempfile::TempDir, std::path::PathBuf, String) {
    61→        let dir = tempfile::TempDir::new().unwrap();
    62→        let path = dir.path().join(format!("tspec{}", SUFFIX));
    63→        std::fs::write(&path, content).unwrap();
    64→
    65→        let mut doc: DocumentMut = content.parse().unwrap();
    66→        edit::validate_key(key).unwrap();
    67→        edit::unset_field(&mut doc, key).unwrap();
    68→        let output = doc.to_string();
    69→        std::fs::write(&path, &output).unwrap();
    70→
    71→        (dir, path, output)
    72→    }
    73→
    74→    #[test]
    75→    fn unset_toplevel_field() {
    76→        let input = "panic = \"abort\"\nstrip = \"symbols\"\n";
    77→        let (_dir, path, _) = unset_in_file(input, "panic");
    78→        let spec = load_spec(&path).unwrap();
    79→        assert_eq!(spec.panic, None);
    80→        assert_eq!(spec.strip, Some(crate::options::StripMode::Symbols));
    81→    }
    82→
    83→    #[test]
    84→    fn unset_nested_field() {
    85→        let input = "[rustc]\nlto = true\nopt_level = \"3\"\n";
    86→        let (_dir, path, _) = unset_in_file(input, "rustc.lto");
    87→        let spec = load_spec(&path).unwrap();
    88→        assert_eq!(spec.rustc.lto, None);
    89→        assert_eq!(spec.rustc.opt_level, Some(crate::types::OptLevel::O3));
    90→    }
    91→
    92→    #[test]
    93→    fn unset_array_field() {
    94→        let input = "[linker]\nargs = [\"-static\"]\n";
    95→        let (_dir, path, _) = unset_in_file(input, "linker.args");
    96→        let spec = load_spec(&path).unwrap();
    97→        assert!(spec.linker.args.is_empty());
    98→    }
    99→
   100→    #[test]
   101→    fn unset_preserves_comments() {
   102→        // Comment is attached to strip (which stays), not to panic (which is removed)
   103→        let input = "panic = \"abort\"\n# Keep this\nstrip = \"symbols\"\n";
   104→        let (_dir, _, output) = unset_in_file(input, "panic");
   105→        assert!(output.contains("# Keep this"));
   106→        assert!(output.contains("strip = \"symbols\""));
   107→    }
   108→
   109→    #[test]
   110→    fn unset_unknown_key_errors() {
   111→        let result = edit::validate_key("nonexistent");
   112→        assert!(result.is_err());
   113→    }
   114→}
   115→

let package_dir = resolve_package_dir(workspace, package)?;\n\n    let output_path = match find_tspec(&package_dir, tspec)? {\n        Some(path) => path,\n        None => bail!(\"no tspec found to modify\"),\n    };\n\n    // Validate key is an array field\n    let kind = edit::validate_key(key)?;\n    if kind != FieldKind::Array {\n        bail!(\"'ts add' only works on array fields, but '{}' is a scalar\", key);\n    }\n\n    // Read, parse, edit, write\n    let content = std::fs::read_to_string(&output_path)\n        .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to read: {}\", output_path.display()))?;\n\n    let mut doc: DocumentMut = content\n        .parse()\n        .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to parse: {}\", output_path.display()))?;\n\n    edit::add_items(&mut doc, key, values, index)?;\n\n    std::fs::write(&output_path, doc.to_string())\n        .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to write: {}\", output_path.display()))?;\n\n    println!(\n        \"Saved {}\",\n        output_path\n            .strip_prefix(workspace)\n            .unwrap_or(&output_path)\n            .display()\n    );\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use (TempDir, std::path::PathBuf, String) {\n        let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();\n        let path = dir.path().join(format!(\"tspec{}\", SUFFIX));\n        std::fs::write(&path, content).unwrap();\n\n        let mut doc: DocumentMut = content.parse().unwrap();\n        edit::add_items(&mut doc, key, values, index).unwrap();\n        let output = doc.to_string();\n        std::fs::write(&path, &output).unwrap();\n\n        (dir, path, output)\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn append_single_to_empty() {\n        let (_dir, path, _) = add_in_file(\"\", \"linker.args\",       let input = \"[linker]\\nargs = [\\\"-static\\\"]\\n\";\n        let (_dir, path, _) =\n            add_in_file(input, \"linker.args\", &vs(&[\"-Wl,--gc-sections\"]), None);\n        let spec =   "content": "//! `tspec ts remove` - Remove items from an array field in a tspec (by value or index)\n\nuse anyhow::{Context, Result, bail};\nuse std::path::Path;\nuse toml_edit::DocumentMut;\n\nuse super::edit::{self, FieldKind};\nuse crate::find_paths::{find_tspec, resolve_package_dir};\n\n/// Remove items from an array field in a tspec (by value or by index)\npub fn remove_value(\n    project_root: &Path,\n    package: Option<&str>,\n    key: &str,\n    values: &[String],\n    index: Option<usize>,\n    tspec: Option<&str>,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    let workspace = project_root;\n    let package_dir = resolve_package_dir(workspace, package)?;\n\n    let output_path = match find_tspec(&package_dir, tspec)? {\n        Some(path) => path,\n        None => bail!(\"no tspec found to modify\"),\n    };\n\n    // Validate key is an array field\n    let kind = edit::validate_key(key)?;\n    if kind != FieldKind::Array {\n        bail!(\n            \"'ts remove' only works on array fields, but '{}' is a scalar\",\n            key\n        );\n    }\n\n    // Validate: either index or values, not both, not neither\n    if index.is_some() && !values.is_empty() {\n        bail!(\"cannot use both --index and values; use --index to remove by position, or provide values to remove by value\");\n    }\n    if index.is_none() && values.is_empty() {\n        bail!(\"provide values to remove, or use --index to remove by position\");\n    }\n\n    // Read, parse, edit, write\n    let content = std::fs::read_to_string(&output_path)\n        .with_context(|| format!(\"failed Some(idx) = index {\n        edit::remove_item_by_index(&mut doc, key, idx)?;\n    } else {\n        edit::remove_items_by_value(&mut doc, key, values)?;\n    }\n\n    std::fs::write(&output_path, .strip_prefix(workspace)\n            .unwrap_or(&output_path)\n            .display()\n    );\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use crate::test_constants::SUFFIX;\n    use s.to_string()).collect()\n    }\n\n    fn remove_in_file(\n        content: &str,\n        key: &str,\n        values: &[String],\n        index: Option<usize>,\n    ) -> (TempDir, std::path::PathBuf, String) {\n        let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();\n        let path = dir.path().join(format!(\"tspec{}\", SUFFIX));\n        std::fs::write(&path, content).unwrap();\n\n        let mut doc: DocumentMut = content.parse().unwrap();\n        if let Some(idx) = index {\n            edit::remove_item_by_index(&mut doc, key, idx).unwrap();\n        } else {\n            edit::remove_items_by_value(&mut doc, key, values).unwrap();\n        }\n        let output = doc.to_string();\n        std::fs::write(&path, &output).unwrap();\n\n        (dir, path, output)\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn remove_by_value() {\n        let input = \"[linker]\\nargs = [\\\"-static\\\", \\\"-nostdlib\\\"]\\n\";\n        let (_dir, path, _) =\n            remove_in_file(input, \"linker.args\", &vs(&[\"-nostdlib\"]), None);\n        let spec = load_spec(&path).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(spec.linker.args, vec![\"-static\".to_string()]);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn remove_multiple_by_value() {\n        let input = \"[linker]\\nargs = [\\\"-static\\\", \\\"-nostdlib\\\", \\\"-Wl,--gc-sections\\\"]\\n\";\n        let (_dir, path, _) = remove_in_file(\n            input,\n            \"linker.args\",\n            &vs(&[\"-static\", \"-Wl,--gc-sections\"]),\n            None,\n        );\n        let spec = load_spec(&path).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(spec.linker.args, vec![\"-nostdlib\".to_string()]);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn remove_last_clears_field() {\n        let input = \"[linker]\\nargs = [\\\"-static\\\"]\\n\";\n        let (_dir, path, _) =\n            remove_in_file(input, \"linker.args\", &vs(&[\"-static\"]), None);\n        let spec = load_spec(&path).unwrap();\n        assert!(spec.linker.args.is_empty());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn remove_by_index() {\n        let input = \"[linker]\\nargs = [\\\"-static\\\", \\\"-nostdlib\\\"]\\n\";\n        let (_dir, path, _) = remove_in_file(input, \"linker.args\", &[], Some(0));\n        let spec = load_spec(&path).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(spec.linker.args, vec![\"-nostdlib\".to_string()]);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn remove_by_index_last_clears_field() {\n        let input = \"[linker]\\nargs = [\\\"-static\\\"]\\n\";\n        let (_dir, path, _) = remove_in_file(input, \"linker.args\", &[], Some(0));\n        let spec = load_spec(&path).unwrap();\n        assert!(spec.linker.args.is_empty());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn remove_nonexistent_value_is_noop() {\n        let input = \"[linker]\\nargs = [\\\"-static\\\"]\\n\";\n        let (_dir, path, _) =\n            remove_in_file(input, \"linker.args\", &vs(&[\"-nostdlib\"]), None);\n        let spec = load_spec(&path).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(spec.linker.args, vec![\"-static\".to_string()]);\n    }\n}\n"
}

project_root: &Path,\n    package: Option<&str>,\n    key: &str,\n    values: &[String],\n    tspec: Option<&str>,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    let workspace = project_root;\n    let package_dir = resolve_package_dir(workspace, package)?;\n\n    // Resolve tspec path (existing or new)\n    let output_path = match find_tspec(&package_dir, tspec)? {\n        Some(path) => path,\n        None => {\n            let base_name = match tspec {\n                Some(t) => t\n                    .strip_suffix(crate::TSPEC_SUFFIX)\n                    .or_else(|| t.strip_suffix(\".toml\"))\n anyhow::bail!(\n                \"scalar field '{}' requires exactly one value, got {}\",\n                key,\n                values.len()\n            );\n        }\n        edit::validate_value(key, &values[0])?;\n    }\n\n    // Read existing content or start empty\n    let content = if output_path.exists() {\n        std::fs::read_to_string(&output_path)\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to read: {}\", output_path.display()))?\n    } else {\n        String::new()\n    };\n\n    // Parse, edit, write\n    let mut doc: DocumentMut = content\n        .parse()\n        .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to parse: {}\", output_path.display()))?;\n\n    edit::set_field(&mut doc, key, values, kind)?;\n\n    std::fs::write(&output_path, .strip_prefix(workspace)\n            .unwrap_or(&output_path)\n            .display()\n    );\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use crate::test_constants::SUFFIX;\n    use Perfect! I found all the relevant sections. Here's a comprehensive summary:

## tspec Management (`ts` subcommands) - README.md Sections

### Main Section: Lines 96-203

**Section Title:** "tspec Management (ts subcommand)" (Line 96)

#### Command Table (Lines 100-109)
- **Line 106:** `ts set` - Set a scalar or replace an entire array (`=`), append to an array (`+=`), or remove from an array (`-=`)
- **Line 107:** `ts unset` - Remove a field (scalar or array) from a tspec entirely

#### Usage Examples (Lines 111-139)

**Set values subsection (Lines 124-129):**
```bash
tspec ts set strip=symbols -p myapp                # Set a scalar field
tspec ts set rustc.lto=true -t tspec-opt           # Set in a specific tspec
tspec ts set 'linker.args=["-static","-nostdlib"]' # Replace entire array with new value
tspec ts set 'linker.args+=-Wl,--gc-sections'      # Append one item to array
tspec ts set 'linker.args-=-nostdlib'               # Remove one item from array
```

**Unset section (Lines 132-133):**
```bash
tspec ts unset rustc.lto -p myapp             # Remove scalar field
tspec ts unset linker.args -t tspec-opt       # Remove array field
```

#### Array Value Rules (Lines 165-201)

**Documentation (Lines 165-178):**
- With brackets `["a","b"]` — TOML inline array. Commas separate items. Quotes protect literal commas.
- Without brackets `value` — always **one item**. The entire value is taken literally.

**Array operation examples (Lines 181-201):**
```bash
# Replace entire array with multiple items (bracket syntax)
tspec ts set 'linker.args=["-static","-nostdlib"]'
tspec ts set 'rustc.build_std=["core","alloc"]'

# Replace entire array with a single item (no brackets needed)
tspec ts set linker.args=-static

# Append to an existing array
tspec ts set 'linker.args+=-nostdlib'              # append one item
tspec ts set 'linker.args+=["-nostdlib","-pie"]'   # append multiple items

# Remove from an array
tspec ts set 'linker.args-=-nostdlib'              # remove one item
tspec ts set 'linker.args-=["-static","-pie"]'     # remove multiple items
```

#### Supported Keys Table (Lines 146-163)
The table documents all valid keys for `ts set`/`ts unset`:
- Array types: `cargo.unstable`, `rustc.build_std`, `rustc.flags`, `linker.args`
- Scalar types: `panic`, `strip`, `cargo.profile`, `cargo.target_triple`, `cargo.target_json`, `cargo.target_dir`, `rustc.opt_level`, `rustc.panic`, `rustc.lto`, `rustc.codegen_units`

#### Implementation Note (Lines 143-144)
`ts set` and `ts unset` use `toml_edit` for surgical editing — comments and formatting are preserved. `ts backup`, `ts restore`, and `ts new -f` use byte-for-byte file copy.
